The clustering kernel is\n// compute-bound, so SMT siblings only contend with each other. An explicit\n// OMP_NUM_THREADS setting is respected as-is.\nstatic int defaultTeamSize() {\n    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") != nullptr) return max_threads;\n    const int siblings = smtSiblingsPerCore();\n    if (siblings > 1 && max_threads == omp_get_num_procs()) {\n        return std::max(1, max_threads / siblings);\n    }\n    return max_threads;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,","originalFile":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - OpenMP Parallel Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization: candidate clusters for all unclustered seed points are\n// generated independently in parallel; the reduction picks the largest\n// cluster with the same tie-breaking order as the sequential version.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Per-thread scratch buffers reused across seeds to avoid repeated allocation\nstruct SeedScratch {\n    std::vector<double> max_dist; // max distance from candidate to cluster members\n    std::vector<char> excluded;   // candidate can no longer join this cluster\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\n//\n// Instead of recomputing the max distance from each candidate to every\n// cluster member on each iteration, the per-candidate max distance is\n// updated incrementally with the distance to the most recently added\n// member. Since the max distance only grows, candidates at or beyond the\n// threshold are excluded permanently. This yields the exact same cluster\n// as the original quadratic scan.\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              SeedScratch& scratch,\n                              std::vector<int>& members) {\n    std::vector<double>& max_dist = scratch.max_dist;\n    std::vector<char>& excluded = scratch.excluded;\n    max_dist.assign(point_count, 0.0);\n    excluded.assign(point_count, 0);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) {\n        if (clustered[i]) excluded[i] = 1;\n    }\n    excluded[seed_point] = 1;\n\n    members.clear();\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    int last_added = seed_point;\n\n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        const Point last = points[last_added];\n        int closest = -1;\n        double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n\n        for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n            if (excluded[candidate]) continue;\n\n            // Update max distance with the distance to the newest member\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], last);\n            double md = max_dist[candidate];\n            if (dist > md) md = dist;\n            max_dist[candidate] = md;\n\n            if (md >= threshold) {\n                excluded[candidate] = 1; // max distance only grows\n                continue;\n            }\n            if (md < min_diameter) {\n                min_diameter = md;\n                closest = candidate;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n\n        excluded[closest] = 1;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n        last_added = closest;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const size_t num_seeds = unclustered_indices.size();\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        size_t best_pos = num_seeds; // position in unclustered_indices\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed, in parallel. Candidate\n        // clusters only read the shared `clustered` state, so all seeds are\n        // independent. Ties in cardinality are broken by the smallest seed\n        // position, matching the sequential iteration order.\n        const int num_threads = static_cast<int>(\n            std::min<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads(), num_seeds));\n        #pragma omp parallel num_threads(num_threads)\n        {\n            SeedScratch scratch;\n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            int local_max = -1;\n            size_t local_pos = num_seeds;\n            int local_seed = -1;\n            std::vector<int> local_members;\n\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic) nowait\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < num_seeds; ++i) {\n                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n\n                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points,\n                                                           threshold, N,\n                                                           scratch, candidate_members);\n\n                if (cardinality > local_max ||\n                    (cardinality == local_max && i < local_pos)) {\n                    local_max = cardinality;\n                    local_pos = i;\n                    local_seed = seed;\n                    local_members.swap(candidate_members);\n                }\n            }\n\n            #pragma omp critical\n            {\n                if (local_max > max_cardinality ||\n                    (local_max == max_cardinality && local_pos < best_pos)) {\n                    max_cardinality = local_max;\n                    best_pos = local_pos;\n                    best_seed = local_seed;\n                    best_cluster_members.swap(local_members);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    // Idle threads spinning on SMT siblings slow down the working threads in\n    // the many short parallel regions; default to passive waiting (set before\n    // the OpenMP runtime initializes; an existing user setting is respected).\n    setenv(\"OMP_WAIT_POLICY\", \"passive\", 0);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n    \n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : \n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n    \n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\", \n           total_clustered, num_points, \n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n    printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n    \n    // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\", \n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; the reduction picks the largest\n// cluster with the same tie-breaking order as the sequential version.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Per-thread scratch buffers reused across seeds to avoid repeated allocation\nstruct SeedScratch {\n    std::vector<double> max_dist; // max distance from candidate to cluster members\n    std::vector<char> excluded;   // candidate can no longer join this cluster\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\n//\n// Instead of recomputing the max distance from each candidate to every\n// cluster member on each iteration, the per-candidate max distance is\n// updated incrementally with the distance to the most recently added\n// member. Since the max distance only grows, candidates at or beyond the\n// threshold are excluded permanently. This yields the exact same cluster\n// as the original quadratic scan.\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              SeedScratch& scratch,\n                              std::vector<int>& members) {\n    std::vector<double>& max_dist = scratch.max_dist;\n    std::vector<char>& excluded = scratch.excluded;\n    max_dist.assign(point_count, 0.0);\n    excluded.assign(point_count, 0);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) {\n        if (clustered[i]) excluded[i] = 1;\n    }\n    excluded[seed_point] = 1;\n\n    members.clear();\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    int last_added = seed_point;\n\n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        const Point last = points[last_added];\n        int closest = -1;\n        double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n\n        for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n            if (excluded[candidate]) continue;\n\n            // Update max distance with the distance to the newest member\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], last);\n            double md = max_dist[candidate];\n            if (dist > md) md = dist;\n            max_dist[candidate] = md;\n\n            if (md >= threshold) {\n                excluded[candidate] = 1; // max distance only grows\n                continue;\n            }\n            if (md < min_diameter) {\n                min_diameter = md;\n                closest = candidate;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n\n        excluded[closest] = 1;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n        last_added = closest;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Number of SMT siblings per physical core (Linux), or 1 if unknown\nstatic int smtSiblingsPerCore() {\n    FILE* f = fopen(\"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/topology/thread_siblings_list\", \"r\");\n    if (!f) return 1;\n    char buf[256];\n    const bool ok = fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr;\n    fclose(f);\n    if (!ok) return 1;\n    // The list is comma-separated with ranges, e.g. \"0,128\" or \"0-1\"\n    int siblings = 0;\n    for (char* p = buf; The clustering kernel is\n// compute-bound, so SMT siblings only contend with each other. An explicit\n// OMP_NUM_THREADS setting is respected as-is.\nstatic int defaultTeamSize() {\n    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") != nullptr) return max_threads;\n    const int siblings = smtSiblingsPerCore();\n    if (siblings > 1 && max_threads == omp_get_num_procs()) {\n        return std::max(1, max_threads / siblings);\n    }\n    return max_threads;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const size_t num_seeds = unclustered_indices.size();\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        size_t best_pos = num_seeds; // position in unclustered_indices\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed, in parallel. Candidate\n        // clusters only read the shared `clustered` state, so all seeds are\n        // independent. Ties in cardinality are broken by the smallest seed\n        // position, matching the sequential iteration order.\n        const int num_threads = static_cast<int>(\n            std::min<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads(), num_seeds));\n        #pragma omp parallel num_threads(num_threads)\n        {\n            SeedScratch scratch;\n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            int local_max = -1;\n            size_t local_pos = num_seeds;\n            int local_seed = -1;\n            std::vector<int> local_members;\n\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic) nowait\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < num_seeds; ++i) {\n                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n\n                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points,\n                                                           threshold, N,\n                                                           scratch, candidate_members);\n\n                if (cardinality > local_max ||\n                    (cardinality == local_max && i < local_pos)) {\n                    local_max = cardinality;\n                    local_pos = i;\n                    local_seed = seed;\n                    local_members.swap(candidate_members);\n                }\n            }\n\n            #pragma omp critical\n            {\n                if (local_max > max_cardinality ||\n                    (local_max == max_cardinality && local_pos < best_pos)) {\n                    max_cardinality = local_max;\n                    best_pos = local_pos;\n                    best_seed = local_seed;\n                    best_cluster_members.swap(local_members);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    // Idle threads spinning on SMT siblings slow down the working threads in\n    // the many short parallel regions; the reduction picks the largest\n// cluster with the same tie-breaking order as the sequential version.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Per-thread scratch buffers reused across seeds to avoid repeated allocation\nstruct SeedScratch {\n    std::vector<double> max_dist; // max distance from candidate to cluster members\n    std::vector<char> excluded;   // candidate can no longer join this cluster\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\n//\n// Instead of recomputing the max distance from each candidate to every\n// cluster member on each iteration, the per-candidate max distance is\n// updated incrementally with the distance to the most recently added\n// member. Since the max distance only grows, candidates at or beyond the\n// threshold are excluded permanently. This yields the exact same cluster\n// as the original quadratic scan.\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              SeedScratch& scratch,\n                              std::vector<int>& members) {\n    std::vector<double>& max_dist = scratch.max_dist;\n    std::vector<char>& excluded = scratch.excluded;\n    max_dist.assign(point_count, 0.0);\n    excluded.assign(point_count, 0);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) {\n        if (clustered[i]) excluded[i] = 1;\n    }\n    excluded[seed_point] = 1;\n\n    members.clear();\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    int last_added = seed_point;\n\n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        const Point last = points[last_added];\n        int closest = -1;\n        double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n\n        for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n            if (excluded[candidate]) continue;\n\n            // Update max distance with the distance to the newest member\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], last);\n            double md = max_dist[candidate];\n            if (dist > md) md = dist;\n            max_dist[candidate] = md;\n\n            if (md >= threshold) {\n                excluded[candidate] = 1; // max distance only grows\n                continue;\n            }\n            if (md < min_diameter) {\n                min_diameter = md;\n                closest = candidate;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n\n        excluded[closest] = 1;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n        last_added = closest;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Number of SMT siblings per physical core (Linux), or 1 if unknown\nstatic int smtSiblingsPerCore() {\n    FILE* f = fopen(\"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/topology/thread_siblings_list\", \"r\");\n    if (!f) return 1;\n    char buf[256];\n    const bool ok = fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr;\n    fclose(f);\n    if (!ok) return 1;\n    // The list is comma-separated with ranges, e.g. \"0,128\" or \"0-1\"\n    int siblings = 0;\n    for (char* p = buf; *p && *p != '\\n';) {\n        char* end;\n        const long a = strtol(p, &end, 10);\n        if (end == p) break;\n        p = end;\n        if (*p == '-') {\n            const long b = strtol(p + 1, &p, 10);\n            siblings += static_cast<int>(b - a + 1);\n        } else {\n            siblings += 1;\n        }\n        if (*p == ',') ++p;\n    }\n    return siblings > 0 ? siblings : 1;\n}\n\n// Default team size: one thread per physical core. The clustering kernel is\n// compute-bound, so SMT siblings only contend with each other. An explicit\n// OMP_NUM_THREADS setting is respected as-is.\nstatic int defaultTeamSize() {\n    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") != nullptr) return max_threads;\n    const int siblings = smtSiblingsPerCore();\n    if (siblings > 1 && max_threads == omp_get_num_procs()) {\n        return std::max(1, max_threads / siblings);\n    }\n    return max_threads;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    const int team_size = defaultTeamSize();\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const size_t num_seeds = unclustered_indices.size();\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        size_t best_pos = num_seeds; // position in unclustered_indices\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed, in parallel. Candidate\n        // clusters only read the shared `clustered` state, so all seeds are\n        // independent. Ties in cardinality are broken by the smallest seed\n        // position, matching the sequential iteration order.\n        const int num_threads = static_cast<int>(\n            std::min<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads(), num_seeds));\n        #pragma omp parallel num_threads(num_threads)\n        {\n            SeedScratch scratch;\n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            int local_max = -1;\n            size_t local_pos = num_seeds;\n            int local_seed = -1;\n            std::vector<int> local_members;\n\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic) nowait\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < num_seeds; ++i) {\n                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n\n                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points,\n                                                           threshold, N,\n                                                           scratch, candidate_members);\n\n                if (cardinality > local_max ||\n                    (cardinality == local_max && i < local_pos)) {\n                    local_max = cardinality;\n                    local_pos = i;\n                    local_seed = seed;\n                    local_members.swap(candidate_members);\n                }\n            }\n\n            #pragma omp critical\n            {\n                if (local_max > max_cardinality ||\n                    (local_max == max_cardinality && local_pos < best_pos)) {\n                    max_cardinality = local_max;\n                    best_pos = local_pos;\n                    best_seed = local_seed;\n                    best_cluster_members.swap(local_members);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    // Idle threads spinning on SMT siblings slow down the working threads in\n    // the many short parallel regions; default to passive waiting (set before\n    // the OpenMP runtime initializes; an existing user setting is respected).\n    setenv(\"OMP_WAIT_POLICY\", \"passive\", 0);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    \n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n    \n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n    \n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n    \n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Per-thread scratch buffers reused across seeds to avoid repeated allocation\nstruct SeedScratch {\n    std::vector<double> max_dist; // max distance from candidate to cluster members\n    std::vector<char> excluded;   // candidate can no longer join this cluster\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\n//\n// Instead of recomputing the max distance from each candidate to every\n// cluster member on each iteration, the per-candidate max distance is\n// updated incrementally with the distance to the most recently added\n// member. Since the max distance only grows, candidates at or beyond the\n// threshold are excluded permanently. This yields the exact same cluster\n// as the original quadratic scan.\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              SeedScratch& scratch,\n                              std::vector<int>& members) {\n    std::vector<double>& max_dist = scratch.max_dist;\n    std::vector<char>& excluded = scratch.excluded;\n    max_dist.assign(point_count, 0.0);\n    excluded.assign(point_count, 0);\n\n    for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) {\n        if (clustered[i]) excluded[i] = 1;\n    }\n    excluded[seed_point] = 1;\n\n    members.clear();\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    int last_added = seed_point;\n\n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        const Point last = points[last_added];\n        int closest = -1;\n        double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n\n        for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n            if (excluded[candidate]) continue;\n\n            // Update max distance with the distance to the newest member\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], last);\n            double md = max_dist[candidate];\n            if (dist > md) md = dist;\n            max_dist[candidate] = md;\n\n            if (md >= threshold) {\n                excluded[candidate] = 1; // max distance only grows\n                continue;\n            }\n            if (md < min_diameter) {\n                min_diameter = md;\n                closest = candidate;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n\n        excluded[closest] = 1;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n        last_added = closest;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Number of SMT siblings per physical core (Linux), or 1 if unknown\nstatic int smtSiblingsPerCore() {\n    FILE* f = fopen(\"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu0/topology/thread_siblings_list\", \"r\");\n    if (!f) return 1;\n    char buf[256];\n    const bool ok = fgets(buf, sizeof(buf), f) != nullptr;\n    fclose(f);\n    if (!ok) return 1;\n    // The list is comma-separated with ranges, e.g. \"0,128\" or \"0-1\"\n    int siblings = 0;\n    for (char* p = buf; *p && *p != '\\n';) {\n        char* end;\n        const long a = strtol(p, &end, 10);\n        if (end == p) break;\n        p = end;\n        if (*p == '-') {\n            const long b = strtol(p + 1, &p, 10);\n            siblings += static_cast<int>(b - a + 1);\n        } else {\n            siblings += 1;\n        }\n        if (*p == ',') ++p;\n    }\n    return siblings > 0 ? siblings : 1;\n}\n\n// Default team size: one thread per physical core. The clustering kernel is\n// compute-bound, so SMT siblings only contend with each other. An explicit\n// OMP_NUM_THREADS setting is respected as-is.\nstatic int defaultTeamSize() {\n    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") != nullptr) return max_threads;\n    const int siblings = smtSiblingsPerCore();\n    if (siblings > 1 && max_threads == omp_get_num_procs()) {\n        return std::max(1, max_threads / siblings);\n    }\n    return max_threads;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    const int team_size = defaultTeamSize();\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const size_t num_seeds = unclustered_indices.size();\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        size_t best_pos = num_seeds; // position in unclustered_indices\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n\n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed, in parallel. Candidate\n        // clusters only read the shared `clustered` state, so all seeds are\n        // independent. Ties in cardinality are broken by the smallest seed\n        // position, matching the sequential iteration order.\n        const int num_threads = static_cast<int>(\n            std::min<size_t>(team_size, num_seeds));\n        #pragma omp parallel num_threads(num_threads)\n        {\n            SeedScratch scratch;\n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            int local_max = -1;\n            size_t local_pos = num_seeds;\n            int local_seed = -1;\n            std::vector<int> local_members;\n\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic) nowait\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < num_seeds; ++i) {\n                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n\n                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points,\n                                                           threshold, N,\n                                                           scratch, candidate_members);\n\n                if (cardinality > local_max ||\n                    (cardinality == local_max && i < local_pos)) {\n                    local_max = cardinality;\n                    local_pos = i;\n                    local_seed = seed;\n                    local_members.swap(candidate_members);\n                }\n            }\n\n            #pragma omp critical\n            {\n                if (local_max > max_cardinality ||\n                    (local_max == max_cardinality && local_pos < best_pos)) {\n                    max_cardinality = local_max;\n                    best_pos = local_pos;\n                    best_seed = local_seed;\n                    best_cluster_members.swap(local_members);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    // Idle threads spinning on SMT siblings slow down the working threads in\n    // the many short parallel regions; default to passive waiting (set before\n    // the OpenMP runtime initializes; an existing user setting is respected).\n    setenv(\"OMP_WAIT_POLICY\", \"passive\", 0);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    \n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n    \n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n    \n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n    \n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n    \n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : \n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n    \n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\", \n           total_clustered, num_points, \n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n    printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n    \n    // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\", \n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);\n        \n        if (valid) {\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return 0;\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":358,"oldLines":11,"newStart":358,"newLines":6,"lines":[" }"," "," int main(int argc, char** argv) {","-    // Idle threads spinning on SMT siblings slow down the working threads in","-    // the many short parallel regions; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011CegNLUJqRBx4Qc3hAb7p8","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The benchmark is now parallelized with OpenMP and produces bit-identical output to the original (verified via the `-r` result hash and `-v` validation across seven size/threshold combinations, including n=4000 against a 25-minute sequential reference run).\n\n**What changed in `qtclustering.cpp`:**\n\n- **Parallel seed evaluation** (the algorithmic hotspot): each round of QT clustering grows an independent candidate cluster from every unclustered seed. These now run in an `omp parallel` region with `schedule(dynamic)`; each thread tracks its local best and a critical section reduces to the global best. Ties in cardinality are broken by the smallest position in the seed list, exactly matching the sequential iteration order, so results are deterministic and identical.\n- **Incremental cluster growth**: the original recomputed the max distance from every candidate to *all* cluster members on every step (O(k²·n) per seed). Now each candidate's max distance is updated only against the newest member, and candidates at/beyond the threshold are permanently excluded (valid because the max only grows). Same exact arithmetic and comparisons, so identical clusters — just O(k·n).\n- **Per-thread scratch buffers** are reused across seeds to avoid allocation churn, and the team size is clamped to the number of remaining seeds.\n- **SMT-aware default team size**: on this 2×64-core EPYC (256 logical CPUs), the default 256 threads were 5× *slower* than 128 because hyperthread siblings contend on the compute-bound kernel and spin-wait. The code now reads the sibling count from sysfs and defaults to one thread per physical core, falling back to `omp_get_max_threads()` if topology is unavailable — and an explicit `OMP_NUM_THREADS` is always respected as-is.\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`:** added `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and linked `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`.\n\n**Performance** (n=4000, t=2.0): original 1,493.6 s → 0.17 s with default threads (~8,800× total; the incremental algorithm alone gives ~100× at 1 thread, and parallel scaling adds the rest). At n=8000, scaling from 1→128 threads is 110.9 s → 0.95 s (~116×, essentially linear).\n\nOne pre-existing quirk I noticed but left untouched: for n ≲ 31 the synthetic data generator infinite-loops (`group_cnt` truncates to 0) — that behavior is identical in the original."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":307,"cache_read_input_tokens":47956,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":307},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"b0f9ceab-2a84-496e-91e6-68da38a3536b","uuid":"603b1fc0-da10-42f7-8f4a-0b1e3c79157d","timestamp":"2026-09-03T10:44:08.662Z","request_id":"req_011CegNLSNWGwVekPEsmGfDc"}
